# Raw Hearthstone non-voice SFX name survey

Scratch findings from a read-only local Unity bundle scan. This is a name-level survey only: no audio payloads were decoded or auditioned, so semantic conclusions remain name-based inferences.

## Environment and totals

| Field | Value |
|---|---:|
| Install path | `C:\Program Files (x86)\Hearthstone` |
| Hearthstone build | `36.2.0.248348` |
| Unity engine fallback | `6000.3.11f1` |
| `.unity3d` bundles discovered/scanned | 4,866 / 4,866 |
| Unreadable bundles | 0 |
| AudioClip objects | 69,959 |
| Distinct AudioClip names | 69,320 |
| Distinct `(name, bundle)` pairs | 69,776 |
| VO-like by documented name/bundle rule | 53,184 |
| Non-VO working partition (includes 97 ambiguous mission clips) | 16,775 |
| Confident non-VO after setting ambiguous mission clips aside | 16,678 |
| Total scan wall time | 125.84 s |

Build evidence: the install has no `.build.info`; both `Hearthstone\.product.db` and `Hearthstone\Accessibility\hsa_manifest.json` contain `36.2.0.248348`. The Unity version string `6000.3.11f1` occurs in `Hearthstone_Data\globalgamemanagers` and matches `Hearthstone.exe` product metadata. The scan used UnityPy 1.25.3; `UnityPy.config.FALLBACK_UNITY_VERSION = '6000.3.11f1'` was set before loads.

### Phase timings

| Phase | Bundles | AudioClips | Unreadable | Seconds |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| sound-named corpus | 576 | 61,314 | 0 | 9.75 |
| remainder | 4,290 | 8,645 | 0 | 116.09 |

The sound-named phase was the exact 576-bundle set whose basename starts with `playsound`, `sound`, `music`, or `heromusic`. The remainder was all other `Data\Win\*.unity3d` files. The remainder contributed 8,645 clips, so limiting the survey to the conventional sound corpus would have missed 12.36% of all AudioClip objects.

## Scan and partition method

1. Enumerated every `Data\Win\*.unity3d` path, sorted by basename.
2. Loaded each bundle independently with UnityPy and selected objects whose Unity type was `AudioClip`.
3. Read only `m_Name`; no `AudioClip.samples`, FSB payload, or decoded waveform was requested.
4. Counted bundle-load errors separately. Result: zero bundle errors and no skipped bundles.
5. Applied the VO-like rule below, then searched only the non-VO working partition for event names.

### VO-like classifier

The classifier is intentionally reproducible and conservative:

- Starts with `VO_`: 52,465 objects.
- Contains `VO` as an underscore-delimited token elsewhere (for example pet names such as `Krush_Emote_Wow_VO_03`): 714 additional objects.
- Remaining object in an `_enus-` localized bundle: 5 additional objects. These five are `Story_06_RehgarMinion_Play_01`, `Story_08_Tyrande_Priestess_Play_01`, `Story_06_RehgarMinion_Attack_01`, `Story_08_Tyrande_Priestess_Attack_01`, and `Hearthstone_Tavern_Abridged_Audio`.
- Total VO-like: 53,184; working non-VO remainder: 16,775.

Important ambiguity: 97 remaining clips are in `soundmission_*_global` and mix obvious sentence/dialogue or boss vocal-style names with mechanical Foley. They are kept in the numeric non-VO remainder so the automatic rule is not silently subjective, but they are isolated as `ambiguous mission vocal/foley` below. Excluding them leaves 16,678 confident non-VO objects. Name inspection alone cannot prove whether boss `Attack`, `Death`, `Intro`, or `EmoteResponse` clips are speech, creature vocals, or Foley.

Bundle-family accounting check: 1,337 + 3,561 + 4,828 + 571 + 234 + 401 + 97 + 5,746 = 16,775.

## Core answer: can game event -> SFX be derived from names alone?

Partly. Names alone are sufficient for several base UI/game-state families, especially `ALERT_YourTurn_0v2`, generic draw/play/drop families, `FX_Secret_Trigger`, and victory/defeat screen cues. They are not sufficient to reconstruct the runtime mix: attacks, impacts, hero powers, hero damage, emotes, legendary plays, skins, and modes have many layered/content-specific clips, and names do not encode which prefab/event selects or combines them.

| Requested event | Name-only result |
|---|---|
| Turn start / your turn | Yes: one exact, generic name. Name alone strongly identifies the alert. |
| End-turn button / timer | Small stable family, but names distinguish animation/state cues rather than one canonical click. |
| Card draw | Small generic variants split player/opponent and animation paths; event family is derivable, exact runtime member is not. |
| Card play / minion drop | Small generic families exist, but play, drop, and summon layers overlap; likely composite. |
| Attack / impact / weapon | Generic launch/impact/hero start/end families exist, plus thousands of card/skin-specific layers. Event family yes; exact mix no. |
| Hero power | No generic activation clip by name. Only icon/flip UI is generic; activation SFX are hero/skin-specific. |
| Game start / mulligan / coin | Small named families for mulligan and coin setup; likely multiple sequential layers rather than one clip. |
| Victory / defeat / game end | Yes: generic base victory/defeat screen and jingle names exist, alongside many mode/skin variants. |
| Damage / death | Death has a generic six-clip minion family. Generic health-damage is not clear; `Shared_Armor_Damage_1` is armor-specific. |
| Secret trigger | Yes: `FX_Secret_Trigger` is exact; birth/play/lock-state companions are separately named. |
| Discover / choice UI | No single generic cue. A few generic-looking choice/discover names coexist with many card-specific reveals. |
| Emote wheel UI | Only `speech_bubble_open/close` look like generic menu UI. No clip name explicitly says emote wheel/radial menu. |

## Raw substring search counts

Counts below are across all 16,775 objects in the working non-VO partition and are case-insensitive. They intentionally overmatch: e.g. `turn` catches `turntable`/`return`, `play` catches `WellPlayed`, and `wheel` catches board clickables. The appendices list the full raw supersets, so every plausible name/bundle pair is preserved for human review rather than discarded by an opaque heuristic.

| Search group | Regex | Object matches | Distinct names | Bundles |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Turn (broad) | `turn` | 67 | 67 | 26 |
| End-turn | `end[ _-]?turn\|turn[ _-]?end` | 8 | 8 | 2 |
| Card draw | `draw` | 29 | 29 | 16 |
| Card play/drop | `play\|drop` | 2,388 | 2,371 | 140 |
| Attack/impact/weapon/swing/hit/whoosh | `attack\|impact\|weapon\|swing\|hit\|whoosh` | 3,621 | 3,604 | 141 |
| Hero power | `hero[ _-]?power` | 343 | 343 | 35 |
| Game start/mulligan/coin | `mulligan\|coin\|game[ _-]?start\|start[ _-]?game` | 169 | 169 | 51 |
| Victory/defeat/game end/stinger | `victory\|defeat\|loss\|lose\|(?:^\|[_ -])win(?:[_ -]\|$)\|game[ _-]?end\|end[ _-]?game\|game[ _-]?over\|stinger` | 1,241 | 1,241 | 91 |
| Damage/hurt | `damag\|hurt` | 54 | 54 | 24 |
| Death/die/dying/kill | `death\|(?:^\|[_ -])die(?:[_ -]\|$)\|dying\|kill` | 2,114 | 2,096 | 122 |
| Secret | `secret` | 9 | 9 | 5 |
| Discover/choice/select/option | `discover\|choice\|choose\|select\|option` | 62 | 62 | 25 |
| Emote/wheel/radial/speech bubble | `emote\|wheel\|radial\|speech[ _-]?bubble` | 215 | 215 | 30 |
